Ruling: model error; p501 p. 19, Relatives who don't have to live with you ("A son or daughter of your brother, sister, half brother, or half sister"; no other descendant of a sibling); p501 p. 19, Joint return: "If you file a joint return, the person can be related to either you or your spouse."; i1040gi p. 21, Multiple support agreements (a rule for a qualifying relative); i1040gi p. 17, Step 1. The reply is invalid in triage (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/dependent.deepseek.json`, state invalid: dependent.dc_count, "collection parent binding mismatch"; dependent.dc_over12_care, "model changed a published input interface"; dependent.edu_line31, "'aqee' has no line 'line31'"), so triage executed nothing. This line and the lines it reads validate; ruled on the merits by reading. Three defects: (1) its dependent.qr_raw puts sibling_descendant (a grandniece or a stepsibling's child, by the relationship question) among the relatives who need not live with you, with no qr_all_year or lawful test, so such a person claimed after a few months in the household is your dependent where the project requires all year; (2) it has no dependent.spouse_relative (BUG-0264), so a relative of your spouse on a joint return who does not live with you is not your dependent (t091.spouse_relative.joint child: relationship other, months 0, spouse_relative yes; project line 19 $500.00, the reply no dependent); (3) it declares multiple_support (with married, divorced_rule, kidnapped, other_claimant) on dependent.claimed itself, so a qualifying child whose support was shared is refused (classm.s3.4 dep_a). It uses qr_all_year as the project does; it has no claimed_no_tin or income_exception refusal (project screens, scope). Project stands.


Ruling: model error; p501 p. 9, Temporary absences: "You and your qualifying person are considered to live together even if one or both of you are temporarily absent from your home due to special circumstances" and "It must be reasonable to assume the absent person will return to the home after the temporary absence." The reply is invalid in triage (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/dependent.deepseek.json`, state invalid: dependent.dc_count, "collection parent binding mismatch"; dependent.dc_over12_care, "model changed a published input interface"; dependent.edu_line31, "'aqee' has no line 'line31'"), so triage executed nothing. This line and the lines it reads validate; ruled on the merits by reading. The line copies dependent.absence_no_return for every entry, so it is yes for a person who is no Test 2 person at all (t091r.absence.roommate_not_test2 room: project no) and needs the answer of every entry, which the interview asks only of a person who may qualify you for head of household (an answer the source does not need). The rule concerns the time the qualifying person lived with you; the project marks only a Test 2 person who counted such an absence (hoh_test2_lived and absence_no_return, BUG-0267). Project stands.

### dependent.hoh_test2 — A Test 2 person who lived with you more than half the year, or of the part of the year a r
Status: **DISAGREE**

Second transcription's note: For a person over age 13 at year end, all 2025 care was after age 12, so the flag follows incapacity and paid expenses; at age 13 the explicit dc_over12_care answer is used.

Ruling: model error; on validation. i2441 p. 3, Column (c): "Check the box in column (c) if the qualifying person listed in column (a) was over age 12 at the time the care was provided and was disabled". The reply is invalid in triage (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/dependent.deepseek.json`, state invalid: dependent.dc_count, "collection parent binding mismatch"; dependent.dc_over12_care, "model changed a published input interface"; dependent.edu_line31, "'aqee' has no line 'line31'"), so triage executed nothing. The line reads dependent.dc_over12_care, which the reply redeclares as a helper over the published input of that id ("model changed a published input interface"); this ruling adjudicates that declaration. By reading, its helper also requires dc_paid above zero for a person over 13 and inherits its dependent.dc_qp (no death-year route). Project stands.

Second:

```json
{"expression": {"unknown": "def line line31"}, "invalid_model_types": [{"line": "dependent.edu_line31", "message": "dependent.dependent.edu_line31: 'aqee' has no line 'line31'"}]}
```

Ruling: model error; on validation. i8863 p. 9, Line 31. The reply is invalid in triage (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/dependent.deepseek.json`, state invalid: dependent.dc_count, "collection parent binding mismatch"; dependent.dc_over12_care, "model changed a published input interface"; dependent.edu_line31, "'aqee' has no line 'line31'"), so triage executed nothing. The line reads line31 of the aqee definition, which has no such line ("'aqee' has no line 'line31'"); this ruling adjudicates the declaration. By reading it would also have no lifetime-learning eligibility test. Project stands.

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
lines:
  - id: dependent.dc_line2d
    printed: "2(d)"
    label: "Qualified expenses you incurred and paid in 2025 for the person listed in column (a)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: dependent.dc_paid}
  - id: dependent.dc_line2c
    printed: "2(c)"
    label: "Check here if the qualifying person was over age 12 and was disabled."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_over12_care, {const: true}]}
    note: "For a person over age 13 at year end, all 2025 care was after age 12, so the flag follows incapacity and paid expenses; at age 13 the explicit dc_over12_care answer is used."
  - id: dependent.edu_line27
    printed: "27"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (see instructions). Don't enter more than $4,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: copy
      from:
        def: aqee
        bind:
          inst: dependent.edu_inst
          mat: dependent.edu_mat
          adj: dependent.edu_adj
        line: line27
  - id: dependent.edu_line28
    printed: "28"
    label: "Subtract $2,000 from line 27. If zero or less, enter -0-."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: copy
      from:
        def: aqee
        bind:
          inst: dependent.edu_inst
          mat: dependent.edu_mat
          adj: dependent.edu_adj
        line: line28
  - id: dependent.edu_line29
    printed: "29"
    label: "Multiply line 28 by 25% (0.25)."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: copy
      from:
        def: aqee
        bind:
          inst: dependent.edu_inst
          mat: dependent.edu_mat
          adj: dependent.edu_adj
        line: line29
  - id: dependent.edu_line30
    printed: "30"
    label: "If line 28 is zero, enter the amount from line 27. Otherwise, add $2,000 to the amount on line 29 and enter the result. Skip line 31."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: copy
      from:
        def: aqee
        bind:
          inst: dependent.edu_inst
          mat: dependent.edu_mat
          adj: dependent.edu_adj
        line: line30
  - id: dependent.edu_line31
    printed: "31"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (see instructions). Include the total of all amounts from all Parts III, line 31, on Part II, line 10."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: copy
      from:
        def: aqee
        bind:
          inst: dependent.edu_inst
          mat: dependent.edu_mat
          adj: dependent.edu_adj
        line: line31
  - id: dependent.one
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Count of this dependent"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: param, ref: dependent.one}
  - id: dependent.qc_rel_age
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying child relationship and age tests"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "child"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling_descendant"}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: qc.age_limit}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.younger, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: qc.student_age_limit}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.student, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.younger, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.disabled, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.qc_raw
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying child tests before tiebreaker and dependency-of-taxpayer rules"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.qc_rel_age, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.self_support, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.joint_return, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [dependent.months, {const: 6, type: Count}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.canada_mexico, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.qr_raw
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying relative tests before multiple-support and dependency-of-taxpayer rules"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "child"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling_descendant"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent_relative"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "in_law"}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - op: test
                  any:
                    - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "household_member"}]}
                    - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "other"}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.qr_all_year, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.lawful, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.qc_raw, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [dependent.gross_income, {param: qr.gross_income_limit}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.supported, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.canada_mexico, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.joint_return, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.other_claimant, {const: false}]}
  - id: dependent.claimed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person can be claimed as a dependent"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.qc_raw, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.qr_raw, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [sa.dependent, {const: false}]}
    refuses:
      - dependent.divorced_rule
      - dependent.kidnapped
      - dependent.married
      - dependent.multiple_support
      - dependent.other_claimant
  - id: dependent.ctc_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying child under age 17 with required SSN for the child tax credit"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.qc_raw, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.tin, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: ctc.age_limit}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.work_ssn, {const: true}]}
    note: "The return-level requirement that the taxpayer and spouse have SSNs valid for employment is not encoded on this per-person line."
  - id: dependent.odc_person
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Dependent qualifying for the credit for other dependents"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.tin, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: false}]}
  - id: dependent.eic_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying child for the earned income credit"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.qc_rel_age, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.joint_return, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.us_half, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.married, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.other_claimant, {const: false}]}
    refuses:
      - dependent.other_claimant
      - dependent.kidnapped
  - id: dependent.eic_ssn_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC qualifying child with an SSN valid for the EIC"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_child, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_ssn, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.dc_under13_care
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Care was provided while the person was under age 13"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_under13, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.dc_qp
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying person for the child and dependent care credit"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.qc_raw, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_under13_care, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_incapable, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, gt: [dependent.months, {const: 6, type: Count}]}
    note: "Encoded for a person who is claimed as a dependent; the disabled-person branch does not consider a person who would be a dependent except for gross income, joint return, or taxpayer dependency."
  - id: dependent.dc_over12_care
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Care was provided when the person was over age 12 and disabled"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, gt: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_incapable, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, gt: [dependent.dc_paid, {const: 0}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_over12_care, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_incapable, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.dc_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of qualifying persons for the child and dependent care credit"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.ptc_magi_counted
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Dependent modified AGI counted for the premium tax credit"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ptc_files, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.ptc_magi
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ptc_files, {const: false}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero_dollars
  - id: dependent.hoh_absence
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Disqualifying absence from the home for head of household"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: copy, from: dependent.absence_no_return}
  - id: dependent.hoh_test1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Head of household Test 1 parent"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.parent_home_paid, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.multiple_support, {const: false}]}
  - id: dependent.hoh_person
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Person who can qualify the taxpayer for head of household Test 2"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.divorced_rule, {const: false}]}
            - op: test
              not:
                op: test
                any:
                  - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "household_member"}]}
                  - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "other"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.multiple_support, {const: false}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.qc_raw, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.married, {const: false}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.qc_raw, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.married, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [sa.dependent, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.hoh_test2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Head of household Test 2"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [household.home_cost, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [dependent.months, {const: 6, type: Count}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.hoh_absence, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.hoh_person, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.ctc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Child tax credit amount for this qualifying child"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.h_ctc_per_child
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: false}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero_money
  - id: dependent.odc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Credit for other dependents amount for this dependent"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.odc_person, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.h_odc_per_dependent
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.odc_person, {const: false}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero_money
  - id: dependent.actc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Additional child tax credit maximum for this qualifying child"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.h_actc_per_child
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: false}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero_money
  - id: dependent.edu_adj
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total adjustments to qualified education expenses"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum
      of:
        - dependent.edu_a2025
        - dependent.edu_a2026
        - dependent.edu_refunds
  - id: dependent.h_ctc_per_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Child tax credit per qualifying child"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ctc.per_child}
  - id: dependent.h_odc_per_dependent
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Credit for other dependents per dependent"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: odc.per_dependent}
  - id: dependent.h_actc_per_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Additional child tax credit per qualifying child"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: actc.per_child}
  - id: dependent.h_zero_money
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero money amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
  - id: dependent.h_zero_dollars
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero signed money amount"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
```
